<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
OK so the 5-epoch model has greedy_f1 ~0.798 for N=8. This doesn't match the 0.7914 claimed in the task either.

Let me look at this from a different angle. The task might have incorrect numbers, or the experiment might have been done by a different worker in a different session and the results are only in memory/artifacts but not on the server. 

I think the best approach now is to:
1. Run the FewNERD N=32 experiment from scratch using the same model as the other N-scaling experiments (the exp_021 model, same as fewnerd_mf4v2_seed42_v3 and fewnerd_n16_s42)


2. Then recompute the unified baseline

Before I run anything, I need to check what the recompute_alpha_crit_unified.py script actually does and whether I should run inference first or can work with existing data. I'm also noticing that the unified_n_scaling_results.txt shows different model outputs for SciERC and CoNLL at N=8, so I need to figure out which model was used for FewNERD's N=8 baseline to keep things consistent.

For FewNERD, the N=8 and N=16 baselines come from fewnerd_mf4v2_seed42 and fewnerd_n16_s42 respectively, but I still need to generate N=32 results. I'm setting up a command to run inference with 32 samples on the FewNERD dataset using the merged checkpoint.

Since this would take about 8 hours on a single GPU, I'm planning to parallelize across 4 GPUs by dividing the 37,648 instances into shards of roughly 9,412 samples each, with each shard running on its own device.

I've confirmed from the previous fewnerd experiment that sharding and merging is supported by the pipeline, so I can run all four shards in parallel and then combine the results afterward.

Looking at the timing from the fewnerd_n16_s456 run, it took about 4 hours to process 37,648 instances with 16 samples each on a single GPU. For the N=32 case with sharding, each shard handles roughly 9,412 instances with 32 samples per instance, which is about half the computational load of the full N=16 run. So I'm estimating each shard will take around 2 hours, meaning the total walltime with parallel execution should be roughly 2 hours as well.

Now I'm ready to start the 4-shard parallel inference run. The main thing to watch is that the `run_mvp_pilot.py` script handles both greedy and sampled decoding in a single pass, where greedy uses temperature=0 regardless of the temperature setting. Since each shard processes its own subset of instances, the greedy predictions will be shard-specific, but when I merge everything together, all the predictions combine correctly.
```

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"#!/usr/bin/env python3\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"Unified MV consistency recomputation (v4).\\nChanges from v3: MV threshold strict > N/2 (N=8: need >=5, not >=4).\\nAlso outputs inclusive (>=4) for v3 comparison.\\nFilters to gold-non-empty instances only.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport json\\nimport argparse\\nfrom collections import Counter\\n\\n\\ndef extract_entities(output_dict):\\n    entities = set()\\n    for e in output_dict.get(\\\"entities\\\", []):\\n        entities.add((e[\\\"text\\\"], e[\\\"type\\\"], e[\\\"start\\\"], e[\\\"end\\\"]))\\n    return frozenset(entities)\\n\\n\\ndef entity_f1_counts(pred, gold):\\n    tp = len(pred & gold)\\n    fp = len(pred - gold)\\n    fn = len(gold - pred)\\n    return tp, fp, fn\\n\\n\\ndef instance_f1(pred, gold):\\n    tp, fp, fn = entity_f1_counts(pred, gold)\\n    if tp == 0:\\n        return 0.0\\n    p = tp / (tp + fp)\\n    r = tp / (tp + fn)\\n    return 2 * p * r / (p + r)\\n\\n\\ndef micro_f1(tp, fp, fn):\\n    if tp == 0:\\n        return 0.0\\n    p = tp / (tp + fp)\\n    r = tp / (tp + fn)\\n    return 2 * p * r / (p + r)\\n\\n\\ndef process_file(path):\\n    greedy_tp = greedy_fp = greedy_fn = 0\\n    mv_strict_tp = mv_strict_fp = mv_strict_fn = 0\\n    mv_incl_tp = mv_incl_fp = mv_incl_fn = 0\\n    oracle_tp = oracle_fp = oracle_fn = 0\\n    n_total = 0\\n    n_gold_nonempty = 0\\n    n_degenerate = 0\\n\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        for line in f:\\n            instance = json.loads(line)\\n            gold_entities = extract_entities(instance[\\\"gold\\\"])\\n            n_total += 1\\n\\n            if len(gold_entities) == 0:\\n                continue\\n\\n            n_gold_nonempty += 1\\n\\n            greedy_entities = extract_entities(instance[\\\"greedy\\\"])\\n            tp, fp, fn = entity_f1_counts(greedy_entities, gold_entities)\\n            greedy_tp += tp; greedy_fp += fp; greedy_fn += fn\\n\\n            samples = instance[\\\"samples\\\"]\\n            n_samples = len(samples)\\n            sample_entity_sets = []\\n            entity_counter = Counter()\\n            for s in samples:\\n                s_entities = extract_entities(s)\\n                sample_entity_sets.append(s_entities)\\n                for e in s_entities:\\n                    entity_counter[e] += 1\\n\\n            threshold = n_samples / 2  # 4.0 for N=8\\n\\n            mv_strict = frozenset(e for e, c in entity_counter.items() if c > threshold)\\n            tp, fp, fn = entity_f1_counts(mv_strict, gold_entities)\\n            mv_strict_tp += tp; mv_strict_fp += fp; mv_strict_fn += fn\\n\\n            mv_incl = frozenset(e for e, c in entity_counter.items() if c >= threshold)\\n            tp, fp, fn = entity_f1_counts(mv_incl, gold_entities)\\n            mv_incl_tp += tp; mv_incl_fp += fp; mv_incl_fn += fn\\n\\n            best_f1_val = -1.0\\n            best_tp = best_fp = best_fn = 0\\n            for s_entities in sample_entity_sets:\\n                f1_val = instance_f1(s_entities, gold_entities)\\n                if f1_val > best_f1_val:\\n                    best_f1_val = f1_val\\n                    best_tp, best_fp, best_fn = entity_f1_counts(s_entities, gold_entities)\\n            oracle_tp += best_tp; oracle_fp += best_fp; oracle_fn += best_fn\\n\\n            if len(set(sample_entity_sets)) == 1:\\n                n_degenerate += 1\\n\\n    g_f1 = micro_f1(greedy_tp, greedy_fp, greedy_fn)\\n    ms_f1 = micro_f1(mv_strict_tp, mv_strict_fp, mv_strict_fn)\\n    mi_f1 = micro_f1(mv_incl_tp, mv_incl_fp, mv_incl_fn)\\n    o_f1 = micro_f1(oracle_tp, oracle_fp, oracle_fn)\\n    degen = n_degenerate / n_gold_nonempty if n_gold_nonempty > 0 else 0\\n\\n    def acrit_old(pi_mv):\\n        return pi_mv / (2 - pi_mv) if pi_mv < 2 else float('inf')\\n\\n    return {\\n        \\\"n_total\\\": n_total,\\n        \\\"n_gold_nonempty\\\": n_gold_nonempty,\\n        \\\"greedy_f1\\\": g_f1,\\n        \\\"mv_strict_f1\\\": ms_f1,\\n        \\\"mv_incl_f1\\\": mi_f1,\\n        \\\"oracle_f1\\\": o_f1,\\n        \\\"delta_sc_strict_pp\\\": (ms_f1 - g_f1) * 100,\\n        \\\"delta_sc_incl_pp\\\": (mi_f1 - g_f1) * 100,\\n        \\\"degen\\\": degen,\\n        \\\"alpha_crit_strict\\\": g_f1 / 2,\\n        \\\"alpha_crit_incl\\\": g_f1 / 2,\\n        \\\"alpha_crit_eff_strict\\\": (g_f1 / 2) / (1 - degen) if degen < 1 else float('inf'),\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.35}}"}
```

mv_strict_fp += fp; mv_strict_fn += fn\\n\\n            mv_incl = frozenset(e for e, c in entity_counter.items() if c >= threshold)\\n            tp, fp, fn = entity_f1_counts(mv_incl, gold_entities)\\n            mv_incl_tp += tp; mv_incl_fp += fp; mv_incl_fn += fn\\n\\n            best_f1_val = -1.0\\n            best_tp = best_fp = best_fn = 0\\n            for s_entities in sample_entity_sets:\\n                f1_val = instance_f1(s_entities, gold_entities)\\n                if f1_val > best_f1_val:\\n                    best_f1_val = f1_val\\n                    best_tp, best_fp, best_fn = entity_f1_counts(s_entities, gold_entities)\\n            oracle_tp += best_tp; oracle_fp += best_fp; oracle_fn += best_fn\\n\\n            if len(set(sample_entity_sets)) == 1:\\n                n_degenerate += 1\\n\\n    g_f1 = micro_f1(greedy_tp, greedy_fp, greedy_fn)\\n    ms_f1 = micro_f1(mv_strict_tp, mv_strict_fp, mv_strict_fn)\\n    mi_f1 = micro_f1(mv_incl_tp, mv_incl_fp, mv_incl_fn)\\n    o_f1 = micro_f1(oracle_tp, oracle_fp, oracle_fn)\\n    degen = n_degenerate / n_gold_nonempty if n_gold_nonempty > 0 else 0\\n\\n    def acrit_old(pi_mv):\\n        return pi_mv / (2 - pi_mv) if pi_mv < 2 else float('inf')\\n\\n    return {\\n        \\\"n_total\\\": n_total,\\n        \\\"n_gold_nonempty\\\": n_gold_nonempty,\\n        \\\"greedy_f1\\\": g_f1,\\n        \\\"mv_strict_f1\\\": ms_f1,\\n        \\\"mv_incl_f1\\\": mi_f1,\\n        \\\"oracle_f1\\\": o_f1,\\n        \\\"delta_sc_strict_pp\\\": (ms_f1 - g_f1) * 100,\\n        \\\"delta_sc_incl_pp\\\": (mi_f1 - g_f1) * 100,\\n        \\\"degen\\\": degen,\\n        \\\"alpha_crit_strict\\\": g_f1 / 2,\\n        \\\"alpha_crit_incl\\\": g_f1 / 2,\\n        \\\"alpha_crit_eff_strict\\\": (g_f1 / 2) / (1 - degen) if degen < 1 else float('inf'),\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.35}}"
    }

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"#!/usr/bin/env python3\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nUnified instance-set N-scaling recomputation.\\nFor each dataset, find instance ID intersection across all N values,\\nthen recompute metrics on this unified set.\\nUses greedy from one reference N (default: smallest N) as unified baseline.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nfrom collections import Counter\\n\\nSCIERC_FILES = {\\n    8: \\\"output/scierc_mf4v2_seed42/samples.jsonl\\\",\\n    16: \\\"output/exp_001_seed42_v2/samples.jsonl\\\",\\n    32: \\\"output/scierc_n32_s42/samples.jsonl\\\",\\n    64: \\\"output/scierc_n64_seed42/samples.jsonl\\\",\\n}\\n\\nCONLL_FILES = {\\n    8: \\\"output/exp002_conll2003/samples.jsonl\\\",\\n    16: \\\"output/exp_002_conll_n16/samples.jsonl\\\",\\n    32: \\\"output/conll_n32_s42/samples.jsonl\\\",\\n    64: \\\"output/conll_n64_seed42/samples.jsonl\\\",\\n}\\n\\n\\ndef extract_entities(output_dict):\\n    entities = set()\\n    for e in output_dict.get(\\\"entities\\\", []):\\n        entities.add((e[\\\"text\\\"], e[\\\"type\\\"], e[\\\"start\\\"], e[\\\"end\\\"]))\\n    return frozenset(entities)\\n\\n\\ndef micro_f1(tp, fp, fn):\\n    if tp == 0:\\n        return 0.0\\n    p = tp / (tp + fp)\\n    r = tp / (tp + fn)\\n    return 2 * p * r / (p + r)\\n\\n\\ndef instance_f1(pred, gold):\\n    tp = len(pred & gold)\\n    fp = len(pred - gold)\\n    fn = len(gold - pred)\\n    if tp == 0:\\n        return 0.0\\n    p = tp / (tp + fp)\\n    r = tp / (tp + fn)\\n    return 2 * p * r / (p + r)\\n\\n\\ndef load_file(path):\\n    instances = {}\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        for line in f:\\n            d = json.loads(line)\\n            instances[d[\\\"id\\\"]] = d\\n    return instances\\n\\n\\ndef compute_metrics(instances, instance_ids, n_samples_to_use, greedy_source=None):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Compute metrics on specified instance subset.\\n    If greedy_source is provided, use greedy from that dict instead of from instances.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    greedy_tp = greedy_fp = greedy_fn = 0\\n    mv_strict_tp = mv_strict_fp = mv_strict_fn = 0\\n    oracle_tp = oracle_fp = oracle_fn = 0\\n    n_degenerate = 0\\n    n_computed = 0\\n\\n    for iid in instance_ids:\\n        inst = instances[iid]\\n        gold_entities = extract_entities(inst[\\\"gold\\\"])\\n        if len(gold_entities) == 0:\\n            continue\\n\\n        n_computed += 1\\n\\n        # Greedy (from reference source if provided)\\n        if greedy_source is not None:\\n            greedy_entities = extract_entities(greedy_source[iid][\\\"greedy\\\"])\\n        else:\\n            greedy_entities = extract_entities(inst[\\\"greedy\\\"])\\n        tp = len(greedy_entities & gold_entities)\\n        fp = len(greedy_entities - gold_entities)\\n        fn = len(gold_entities - greedy_entities)\\n        greedy_tp += tp; greedy_fp += fp; greedy_fn += fn\\n\\n        # Samples\\n        samples = inst[\\\"samples\\\"][:n_samples_to_use]\\n        n_s = len(samples)\\n        entity_counter = Counter()\\n        sample_entity_sets = []\\n        for s in samples:\\n            s_entities = extract_entities(s)\\n            sample_entity_sets.append(s_entities)\\n            for e in s_entities:\\n                entity_counter[e] += 1\\n\\n        # MV strict: > N/2\\n        threshold = n_s / 2\\n        mv_strict = frozenset(e for e, c in entity_counter.items() if c > threshold)\\n        tp = len(mv_strict & gold_entities)\\n        fp = len(mv_strict - gold_entities)\\n        fn = len(gold_entities - mv_strict)\\n        mv_strict_tp += tp; mv_strict_fp += fp; mv_strict_fn += fn\\n\\n        # Oracle\\n        best_f1_val = -1.0\\n        best_tp = best_fp = best_fn = 0\\n        for s_entities in sample_entity_sets:\\n            f1_val = instance_f1(s_entities, gold_entities)\\n            if f1_val > best_f1_val:\\n                best_f1_val = f1_val\\n                btp = len(s_entities & gold_entities)\\n                bfp = len(s_entities - gold_entities)\\n                bfn = len(gold_entities - s_entities)\\n                best_tp, best_fp, best_fn = btp, bfp, bfn\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.35}}"}

      "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"#!/usr/bin/env python3\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nUnified instance-set N-scaling recomputation.\\nFor each dataset, find instance ID intersection across all N values,\\nthen recompute metrics on this unified set.\\nUses greedy from one reference N (default: smallest N) as unified baseline.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nfrom collections import Counter\\n\\nSCIERC_FILES = {\\n    8: \\\"output/scierc_mf4v2_seed42/samples.jsonl\\\",\\n    16: \\\"output/exp_001_seed42_v2/samples.jsonl\\\",\\n    32: \\\"output/scierc_n32_s42/samples.jsonl\\\",\\n    64: \\\"output/scierc_n64_seed42/samples.jsonl\\\",\\n}\\n\\nCONLL_FILES = {\\n    8: \\\"output/exp002_conll2003/samples.jsonl\\\",\\n    16: \\\"output/exp_002_conll_n16/samples.jsonl\\\",\\n    32: \\\"output/conll_n32_s42/samples.jsonl\\\",\\n    64: \\\"output/conll_n64_seed42/samples.jsonl\\\",\\n}\\n\\n\\ndef extract_entities(output_dict):\\n    entities = set()\\n    for e in output_dict.get(\\\"entities\\\", []):\\n        entities.add((e[\\\"text\\\"], e[\\\"type\\\"], e[\\\"start\\\"], e[\\\"end\\\"]))\\n    return frozenset(entities)\\n\\n\\ndef micro_f1(tp, fp, fn):\\n    if tp == 0:\\n        return 0.0\\n    p = tp / (tp + fp)\\n    r = tp / (tp + fn)\\n    return 2 * p * r / (p + r)\\n\\n\\ndef instance_f1(pred, gold):\\n    tp = len(pred & gold)\\n    fp = len(pred - gold)\\n    fn = len(gold - pred)\\n    if tp == 0:\\n        return 0.0\\n    p = tp / (tp + fp)\\n    r = tp / (tp + fn)\\n    return 2 * p * r / (p + r)\\n\\n\\ndef load_file(path):\\n    instances = {}\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        for line in f:\\n            d = json.loads(line)\\n            instances[d[\\\"id\\\"]] = d\\n    return instances\\n\\n\\ndef compute_metrics(instances, instance_ids, n_samples_to_use, greedy_source=None):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Compute metrics on specified instance subset.\\n    If greedy_source is provided, use greedy from that dict instead of from instances.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    greedy_tp = greedy_fp = greedy_fn = 0\\n    mv_strict_tp = mv_strict_fp = mv_strict_fn = 0\\n    oracle_tp = oracle_fp = oracle_fn = 0\\n    n_degenerate = 0\\n    n_computed = 0\\n\\n    for iid in instance_ids:\\n        inst = instances[iid]\\n        gold_entities = extract_entities(inst[\\\"gold\\\"])\\n        if len(gold_entities) == 0:\\n            continue\\n\\n        n_computed += 1\\n\\n        # Greedy (from reference source if provided)\\n        if greedy_source is not None:\\n            greedy_entities = extract_entities(greedy_source[iid][\\\"greedy\\\"])\\n        else:\\n            greedy_entities = extract_entities(inst[\\\"greedy\\\"])\\n        tp = len(greedy_entities & gold_entities)\\n        fp = len(greedy_entities - gold_entities)\\n        fn = len(gold_entities - greedy_entities)\\n        greedy_tp += tp; greedy_fp += fp; greedy_fn += fn\\n\\n        # Samples\\n        samples = inst[\\\"samples\\\"][:n_samples_to_use]\\n        n_s = len(samples)\\n        entity_counter = Counter()\\n        sample_entity_sets = []\\n        for s in samples:\\n            s_entities = extract_entities(s)\\n            sample_entity_sets.append(s_entities)\\n            for e in s_entities:\\n                entity_counter[e] += 1\\n\\n        # MV strict: > N/2\\n        threshold = n_s / 2\\n        mv_strict = frozenset(e for e, c in entity_counter.items() if c > threshold)\\n        tp = len(mv_strict & gold_entities)\\n        fp = len(mv_strict - gold_entities)\\n        fn = len(gold_entities - mv_strict)\\n        mv_strict_tp += tp; mv_strict_fp += fp; mv_strict_fn += fn\\n\\n        # Oracle\\n        best_f1_val = -1.0\\n        best_tp = best_fp = best_fn = 0\\n        for s_entities in sample_entity_sets:\\n            f1_val = instance_f1(s_entities, gold_entities)\\n            if f1_val > best_f1_val:\\n                best_f1_val = f1_val\\n                btp = len(s_entities & gold_entities)\\n                bfp = len(s_entities - gold_entities)\\n                bfn = len(gold_entities - s_entities)\\n                best_tp, best_fp, best_fn = btp, bfp, bfn\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.35}}"

=== Table 1: Per-N greedy (shows the inconsistency) ===\\n    p(f\\\"\\\\n  --- Per-N own greedy (showing baseline drift) ---\\\")\\n    p(f\\\"  {'N':>4} | {'Greedy F1 (own)':>15}\\\")\\n    p(f\\\"  {'-'*4}-+-{'-'*15}\\\")\\n    for n in sorted(all_data.keys()):\\n        m = compute_metrics(all_data[n], gold_nonempty_ids, n, greedy_source=None)\\n        p(f\\\"  {n:>4} | {m['greedy_f1']:>15.4f}\\\")\\n\\n    # === Table 2: Unified greedy baseline ===\\n    p(f\\\"\\\\n  --- Unified baseline (greedy from N={ref_n}) ---\\\")\\n    hdr = f\\\"  {'N':>4} | {'Greedy F1':>10} | {'MV strict F1':>12} | {'MV Δ(pp)':>9} | {'Degen%':>7} | {'Oracle F1':>10} | {'#inst':>6}\\\"\\n    sep = f\\\"  {'-'*4}-+-{'-'*10}-+-{'-'*12}-+-{'-'*9}-+-{'-'*7}-+-{'-'*10}-+-{'-'*6}\\\"\\n    p(hdr)\\n    p(sep)\\n\\n    results = {}\\n    for n in sorted(all_data.keys()):\\n        m = compute_metrics(all_data[n], gold_nonempty_ids, n, greedy_source=greedy_ref)\\n        results[n] = m\\n        p(f\\\"  {n:>4} | {m['greedy_f1']:>10.4f} | {m['mv_strict_f1']:>12.4f} | {m['mv_delta_pp']:>+9.2f} | {m['degen_pct']:>6.1f}% | {m['oracle_f1']:>10.4f} | {m['n_instances']:>6}\\\")\\n\\n    return results, lines\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    os.chdir(\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie\\\")\\n\\n    all_lines = []\\n    all_results = {}\\n\\n    for key, name, fmap in [\\n        (\\\"scierc\\\", \\\"SciERC Finetuned (seed=42)\\\", SCIERC_FILES),\\n        (\\\"conll\\\", \\\"CoNLL-2003 Finetuned (seed=42)\\\", CONLL_FILES),\\n    ]:\\n        results, lines = process_dataset(name, fmap)\\n        all_results[key] = results\\n        all_lines.extend(lines)\\n\\n    output_path = \\\"output/unified_n_scaling_results.txt\\\"\\n    with open(output_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        f.write(\\\"\\\\n\\\".join(all_lines) + \\\"\\\\n\\\")\\n\\n    print(f\\\"\\\\n\\\\nResults saved to {output_path}\\\")\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.35}}"
